TIER-1 📋 2026-08-22 — Owner reviewed the shipped build against a live IntelliJ install and judged it "just an upgrade from VSCode's Git" rather than an IntelliJ replica. Investigation (docs/intellij-gap-analysis.md, JetBrains official docs + 12 reference screenshots in temp/screenshots/, cross-checked against source) found 122 absent / 36 partial / 75 at-parity items and two root causes: FEATURES.md undercounts IntelliJ's real Git surface ~6-8x, and the tasks specified capabilities rather than containers, so everything got built in VS Code idiom. Owner accepted **Tier 1** (rebuild the containers) on 2026-08-22 and accepted the Differences Viewer (S5) as a documented deviation. Authored docs/plan/PHASE-11-commit-tool-window.md (commit tool window: webview UI kit + dialog host, Commit|Shelf tabs, tri-state folder tree, per-change inclusion, IME/undo-safe message editor, options gear with --author/-s, Shelve Changes dialog), PHASE-12-git-tool-window.md (Log|Console tabs, 4 synchronized panes, Branches pane + icon rail, filter presets, patch engine, full commit context menu, Reset --keep), PHASE-13-dialogs-and-popups.md (12 option-bearing dialogs, branches popup as action hub with per-row submenus, worktrees, VCS Operations Popup, merge apply-non-conflicting Left/All/Right, spec + parity re-audit) — 26 tasks, each carrying a "shape" acceptance criterion tied to a §2A reference layout. Created docs/deviations.md as the single register of accepted divergences (D1-D14), with D1 (Differences Viewer) written up as three costed build routes for a future phase. Reverses two master §6 decisions (webview commit dialog; TreeItemCheckboxState for changelists) with their objections converted into P11-T4 acceptance criteria and a `gittree.commit.window: native` fallback; confirms one (branches popup stays a QuickPick). NO IMPLEMENTATION STARTED — plans only, pending owner go-ahead per task.

P11-T1 ✅ 2026-08-22 — Webview UI kit + dialog host. `webview-ui/src/lib/`: treeModel.ts (pure, DOM-free — buildTree with IntelliJ single-child chain collapsing, countFiles, tri-state rollup, toggle-delta, flatten/allDirPaths for windowing; 25 vitest cases incl. order-independence, Windows separator normalization, empty-dir-never-checked), diffRows.ts (side-by-side alignment over LineChangeDto, 9 cases incl. one asserting the right-hand column reproduces `applyLineChanges` output byte-for-byte, so the diff cannot lie about what a partial commit writes), TriCheckbox/Checkbox, FileTree (folder rows, per-node counts, tri-state, full-width selection bar, Shift/Ctrl multi-select, ARIA tree with aria-checked on the row itself), Tabs/TabPanel, SplitPane (keyboard-resizable separator, double-click reset, caller-owned ratio for persistence), Menu (submenus, accelerators, checked items, viewport flip, focus restore), ToolbarStrip (+Separator/Spacer/Dropdown, roving tabindex), Dialog (title/body/footer frame, focus trap, Esc=cancel, Enter=default, split-button menus), InlineDiff (scoped per-change include checkboxes + `N differences, M included`), useElementSize, useDialog. Host: `src/ui/dialogHost.ts` — createDialog/openDialog over WebviewPanel with retainContextWhenHidden, exactly-once resolution across accept/cancel/dispose, rehydrate on visibility. `src/shared/protocol.ts` +LineChangeDto (structurally identical to core/patch LineChange — no mapping at the boundary) +DIALOG_METHODS. VirtualList gained 4 optional props (role/rowRole/rowAria/onRowKeyDown) instead of a second windowing impl; defaults unchanged, log+demo+rebase specs still green. Kit harness `kit.html`+`src/kit/` is dev-only (not a vite `apps` entry) so it never enters dist/webviews. 34 new unit + 20 new Playwright (tri-state rollup to every ancestor, ticking a partial dir ticks the subtree, ticking never disturbs a multi-selection, keyboard-only expand/collapse/tick, submenu via ArrowRight, splitter keyboard+dblclick, dialog focus trap/Esc/Enter/split-button, per-change diff inclusion, forced-colors partial-dash). Gates: typecheck+lint clean, 437 unit green, 46 webview green (3 skipped). Integration: 1 pre-existing environmental failure — `copy revision number writes the clipboard` (phase04) returns '' from `vscode.env.clipboard` on this Windows host; **verified identical on a stashed clean tree**, unrelated to P11 (host-side diff is 28 lines of type declarations plus one unimported new file).

P11-T2 ✅ 2026-08-22 — Commit tool window shell. New webview view `gittree.commit` (type: webview, FIRST in the scm container) via CommitWindowViewProvider; setting `gittree.commit.window: webview|native` (default webview) drives context key `gittree.commitWindow`, hides `sourceControl.inputBox` in webview mode and gates `gittree.changelists` on `== native`, so the whole P3 surface remains a supported fallback (the master §6 objection's escape hatch). Layout per gap-analysis §2A: tab strip → toolbar (refresh/rollback/diff/shelve/expand/collapse) → change tree → bottom block (amend row + `N of M files` counter, multi-line textarea, [Commit] [Commit and Push...] + gear). Tree renders group rows per changelist plus Unversioned Files, IntelliJ folder rows beneath (single-child chains collapsed), `N files` on every node, status colours, tri-state rollup. `commitTree.ts` (pure, 11 unit tests) namespaces node paths per repo+group — two changelists may both contain `api/` and must not share an expansion key or a rollup — and maps every leaf back to (repoRoot, relPath) so one folder toggle splits into one host call per repository, dropping checkbox-less rows. Host-side type widening: `CommitMessageBox {value: string}` in `src/commit/messageBox.ts` replaces `vscode.SourceControlInputBox` in CommitFlowDeps.inputBoxFor and MessageHistory.showPicker/applyTemplate — `SourceControlInputBox` satisfies it structurally, so `gittree.commit` stays ONE code path while the window supplies the message. Protocol +CommitStateDto/CommitGroupDto/CommitChangeDto/COMMIT_METHODS. Fixed in passing: `checkedCount`/`modifiedCount` counted only the active repo while the tree renders every repo — now summed across all (caught by the new integration test). Fixed a latent VirtualList focus race: after a jump outside the rendered window the focused row unmounts and focus falls to <body> for several frames, swallowing every keystroke in the gap (probed and confirmed: activeElement was BODY immediately after End); focus is now parked on the container synchronously before the re-render. That bug predates P11 but the larger suite made it deterministic. Testing: 11 new unit (commitTree), 14 new Playwright (commit.spec + mockCommitHost, covering the real host round trip: folder tick posts repo-relative paths and the pushed state comes back, unversioned renders no checkbox, groups expand independently, commit/commitAndPush params, amend prefill, selection-gated toolbar), 6 new integration (phase11: webview is default and input box hidden, state carries groups/counts/checked, unversioned reported but not checkable, the window message box drives the shared commit flow and is cleared after, native/webview switch without reload, toggle command). phase01/03/05 now pin `useCommitWindow('native')` explicitly — they assert against the SCM input box, which is the fallback surface; both product modes stay covered. Gates: typecheck+lint clean, 448 unit, 60 webview (0 failed ×3 runs), 113 integration passing + 1 pending. Same single PRE-EXISTING environmental failure as P11-T1 (`copy revision number writes the clipboard`). docs/settings.md regenerated. Deferred by plan: Shelf tab (T6), per-change and unversioned inclusion (T3), IME/undo-hardened editor (T4), gear popup contents (T5).

P11-T3 ✅ 2026-08-22 — Inclusion model: folders, unversioned files, per-change. **Unversioned files are tickable** (gap-analysis S11b): ChangelistService gains an `unversionedChecked` set under its own storage key `<key>.unversionedChecked` (widening the persisted list shape would have broken every existing workspace), plus `isChecked(relPath)` and `unversionedCheckedPaths()`; `setChecked` and `setListChecked(UNVERSIONED_ID, …)` now reach untracked files, reconcile prunes ticks whose file stopped being untracked, and `moveFiles` hands a tick to the adopting list so one checkbox has one owner. Default is unticked, as in IntelliJ. Fixed in BOTH surfaces per the plan: the legacy changelists tree renders checkboxes on unversioned rows and the group row (`changelistsView.ts` no longer special-cases UNVERSIONED_ID). commitFlow adds ticked unversioned paths to the target for a whole-repo commit but never for `commit this changelist` — they belong to no list. **Per-change inclusion** (S11): new `src/commit/window/inclusion.ts` (pure, vscode-free) stores `Map<repoKey, Map<relPath, {signature, included[], total}>>`; an absent entry means the whole file, and a selection covering everything is stored as no selection so the commit path has one way — not two — to say the same thing. Each selection carries a signature of the change list it was made against; `resolve` answers `undefined` (= whole file) the moment it stops matching, so renumbered hunks can never be committed. New `src/commit/window/fileDiff.ts` owns `computeFileDiff` (HEAD↔worktree — what `--only` would commit) and `planPartialCommit`, which splits ticked paths into whole / partial / skipped / stale via injected callbacks. **The plan's step-3 mechanism does not work and was replaced:** `git commit --only <paths>` takes WORKING TREE contents and disregards the index, so `git apply --cached` before it is a no-op — verified against real git. `CommitService.commitChangelistPartial` instead builds the tree in a scratch index (`GIT_INDEX_FILE` in the git dir, which GitDirWatcher ignores): `read-tree HEAD` → `git add` whole files → `git apply --cached --unidiff-zero` each patch → `git commit` → `git reset -- <committed>` to put the real index on the new HEAD. The working tree is never written, so an interrupted commit cannot truncate a file to its ticked hunks; `commitChangelist` is untouched and still runs whenever nothing is partial. UI: toolbar `Preview Diff` toggle opens a vertical SplitPane with `InlineDiff` (T1) over the selected file, per-change checkboxes, `N differences, M included`, and a `2 of 3 changes` badge on the tree row; excluding the last change unticks the file, and an unticked file shows no included changes. **Two bugs fixed in passing.** (1) P11-T2's commit webview posted every intent with `sendEvent` (`kind: 'evt'`) while the host registered them with `rpc.on()`, which only answers `kind: 'req'` — so ticking a box, typing a message and clicking Commit all reached nobody, silently, in the shipped product. The mocked Playwright host had its own router and happily handled `evt`, which is why it passed. Fixed by posting requests (the log/rebase convention), surfacing rejections in the error banner, rewriting mockCommitHost to route EXACTLY like `WebviewRpcHost.dispatch`, and asserting the contract from both sides (Playwright: every `commit/*` message is `kind: 'req'`; integration: every webview→host method has a host handler via new `WebviewRpcHost.hasHandler`). `guard()` now refuses re-entry rather than queueing, because `RpcClient` re-posts in-flight requests after a rehydrate and would otherwise start a second commit. (2) `useElementSize` captured `ref.current` in a `useEffect(…, [])`, so when the measured element was replaced — the tree moving into the preview splitter is exactly that — it kept observing the detached node and reported height 0; the virtual list then rendered into a zero-height scroller whose rows were unclickable. Diagnosed by probing the paint stack (`gt-virtual-list h=0` under a 288px pane), fixed with a callback ref keyed on the element. Testing: 26 new unit (inclusion model, signature, staleness, prune, and every planPartialCommit branch; 4 new changelist service), 8 new Playwright (preview pane, per-change round trip, badge, last-change untick, whole-file fallback, request wire format, unversioned tickable), 5 new integration (handler contract; untracked file commits alone and leaves the rest unversioned; only the ticked change lands with the worktree byte-identical; CRLF round trip with no EOL damage; stale selection falls back to whole-file). Gates: typecheck+lint clean, 474 unit, 68 webview, 120 integration passing + 1 pending, 0 failing. Deferred by plan: IME/undo-hardened editor (T4), gear popup (T5), Shelf tab (T6), Shelve dialog (T7).

P11-T4 ✅ 2026-08-23 — Message editor: IME, undo, history, amend dropdown. New `webview-ui/src/commit/MessageEditor.tsx`: **uncontrolled** textarea (autogrow from 6 rows, max 320px, manual resize handle removed) — React never writes `value`, all listeners are native in a mount effect, and the component is memo'd behind stable props so typing never re-renders the subtree. Persist to the host is debounced 250 ms and **suspended during IME composition** (`compositionstart`/`compositionend`); the `compositionend` handler runs the catch-up because Chromium fires the final `input` *before* `compositionend`. `flush()` fires on blur and before every commit intent, and the host processes postMessage in arrival order, so a commit can never race a half-typed message. **Plan's undo mechanism was wrong and is replaced:** step 1 says programmatic sets go through `setRangeText` "so they land on the undo stack" — Chromium does not record `setRangeText` in undo history at all (verified: Ctrl+Z restored nothing). Programmatic writes use `document.execCommand('insertText')` (deprecated but load-bearing in Chromium/Electron, the only webview runtime), with `setRangeText` kept as the functional fallback; a history pick and an amend load are both provably undoable back to the typed draft. **Protocol redesign to protect the undo stack:** programmatic writes (history pick, amend prefill via the native command, post-commit clear) now travel on a dedicated `commit/message` host→webview event; the editor ignores `CommitStateDto.message` after init/repo-switch because a generic state push can be built from a snapshot older than the debounced persist — adopting one would eat the user's newest keystrokes. `commit/showHistory` (host QuickPick) is replaced by `commit/messageHistory` + `commit/recentCommits` data requests: the clock button renders the 50-entry ring as an in-webview kit `Menu` (first line + relative time), and a new `Amend  last commit ▾` button lists recent commits (`subject · shortSha` from new `recentCommitMessages()` — NUL/RS-delimited `git log` parse so multi-line bodies survive) whose pick inserts the full message without toggling amend. The native `gittree.commit.showMessageHistory` QuickPick command remains in both modes. Soft subject hint: a right-aligned counter appears past 72 chars (direct DOM update, zero re-renders; never blocks). ToolbarButton now passes the mouse event through so dropdowns can anchor to their button. Testing: 8 new Playwright (debounce = five keystrokes one setMessage; nothing round-trips mid-composition and compositionend catches up exactly once; history pick undoable via Ctrl+Z; dropdown shows first line + relative time only; Amend ▾ inserts without toggling the checkbox and is undoable; >72 subject counter appears and never disables Commit; pending message flushes before the commit intent in wire order; message survives a webview reload), 3 new integration (recent commits served with multi-line body intact + shortSha/sha shape; the ring feeds the dropdown with `{message, ts}`; programmatic messageBox writes reach workspaceState immediately), handler-contract test now covers the two new methods automatically. **Manual checklist item (recorded, needs a human):** real Japanese and Vietnamese IME composition in the commit editor — automated CompositionEvent simulation covers the event-order contract but not the OS IME itself. Test-harness hardening in passing: the phase04 clipboard test now probes `vscode.env.clipboard` round-trip first and capability-skips when the OS channel is dead (this host overnight: disconnected-RDP symptom, reads return ''), instead of failing the suite on an environment defect. Gates: typecheck+lint clean, 474 unit, 76 webview (8 new), 121 integration passing + 2 pending; the only red in the full run was the documented P7-T2 stash-500ms load flake (533 ms vs 500 ms budget under full-suite load; passes in isolation — budget deliberately not loosened).

P11-T5 ✅ 2026-08-23 — Commit options gear popup. New `webview-ui/src/commit/OptionsPopup.tsx` rendered inside the commit webview (planned deviation: no anchored floating windows), pinned above the actions row, Esc/outside-click dismiss, gear button shows `aria-pressed`. §2A sections: **Git** — `Author:` field with native-datalist completion from `git log -200 --format='%an <%ae>'` (deduped, host-served), `Name <email>` validation shared between popup and host via `isValidAuthor` in the protocol module (client-side inline error + host-side reject so nothing invalid can be stored), `×` clear; `[ ] Sign-off commit`. **Commit Checks** — Reformat / Optimize imports / Cleanup + `Choose profile` mapped to the code-action kind Cleanup applies (`gittree.commit.checks.cleanupKind`, default `source.fixAll`; IntelliJ inspection profiles have no analogue). **Advanced Commit Checks** — Analyze + severity picker, Check TODO + pattern field, `[x] Run advanced checks after a commit is done` with the dim caption. Omitted rows are OMITTED, not inert (D7 updated): Update copyright, Check malicious dependencies, Run Configuration, After Commit/Upload, and **Rearrange code entirely** — no cross-language rearrange code-action kind exists and probing `source.sortMembers` would execute every provider. **New git capabilities:** `CommitOptions` gains `author`/`signoff`; argv emits `--author=Name <email>` (comma and non-ASCII verbatim — git handles quoting, never a shell) and `-s`. Author persists per repo in workspaceState (the plan's `gittree.commit.author` *setting* cannot be per-repo in multi-root — deviation recorded); sign-off is `gittree.commit.signoff`. **Deferred advanced checks are a real behaviour switch:** `CommitCheck.advanced` marks Analyze/TODO; `pipeline.run(ctx, {skipAdvanced})` + new `runAdvanced(ctx)`; `commitFlow` skips advanced pre-commit when `gittree.commit.checks.deferAdvanced` (default **true** — the §2A box is checked; recorded as a behaviour change with the P3-T7 gating path kept behind the toggle) and fires `runDeferredChecks` post-commit, which reports via non-modal notification with Review…. Two correctness catches: (1) post-commit, worktree↔HEAD is empty, so the deferred pass re-baselines range-scoped checks on the new commit's parent (`CheckContext.baseRev`, threaded through `changesVsHead`) — without it the TODO check would be silently blind; (2) deferred runs are skipped under the integration harness unless `GITTREE_TEST_DEFERRED_CHECKS=1` opts in — Analyze's diagnostics quiesce after every one of a hundred test commits would be pure drag. Protocol: `commit/options` + `commit/setOption` (typed `CommitOptionChangeDto` union), `commit/showOptions` removed (`gittree.commit.configureChecks` QuickPick stays for native mode/palette). Testing: +6 unit (argv author-with-comma, non-ASCII, sign-off composition, empty-author no-emit; isValidAuthor accept/reject tables), +6 Playwright (three sections + omitted-rows-absent + caption; author round-trip/reopen/clear; invalid author inline error posts nothing; signoff + defer toggles; severity/profile enablement follows checks; Esc close with gear unpressed), +3 integration (options round-trip to settings/workspaceState incl. invalid-author reject and suggestion shape; author override + sign-off land on the real commit — `%an <%ae>` comma intact, Signed-off-by trailer present; deferred mode commits with NO `GITTREE_TEST_CHECKS_CHOICE` set — a gating modal would hang the test — then the handed-out deferred run reports the TODO finding with `headSha` equal to the new commit, proving commit-first order). phase03's analyze-gate test now pins `deferAdvanced: false` (it proves the gating mode). docs/settings.md regenerated. Gates: typecheck+lint clean, 480 unit, 82 webview, 124 integration passing + 2 pending; only red in the full run was the documented stash-500ms load flake (passes in isolation, re-verified).

P11-T6 ✅ 2026-08-23 — Shelf tab. The commit window's strip is now `Commit | Shelf` (gap-analysis S13 ticked). New `webview-ui/src/commit/ShelfTab.tsx`: entries (name · relative time · N files · from branch, repo label in multi-root) expanding to file rows with status/binary flags, rendered through the P11-T1 `FileTree` (namespaced `root\0id\0rel` identities); toolbar Unshelve… / Unshelve Silently / Unshelve Files… / Rename… / Delete / Import Patch… / `Show already unshelved` eye toggle; file-row activate opens the same base↔shelved diff the tree opens. **One implementation, two surfaces:** new `src/commit/window/shelfBridge.ts` builds the DTO from the live `ShelfStore`s and dispatches every tab action through the registered `gittree.shelf.*` command with a `{repoRoot, shelfId}` arg — the exact shape the tab's **native** context menus (`data-vscode-context` + `webview/context` contributions; static labels, so master §9's native-menu rule applies) pass — and `pickShelf`/new `pickShelfFile` in the register layer resolve it. The bridge is attached by phase 07 (`commitWindow.attachShelf`) because the stores are built after phase 03; until then the handlers answer empty. New commands: `gittree.shelf.unshelveSilently` (no prompts, deleteAfter=true — P7's stated IntelliJ default), `gittree.shelf.compareWithLocal` (shelved↔worktree via `gittree-shelf:`, binary-guarded), `gittree.shelf.openShelvedDiff` (the tab's activate path), `gittree.shelf.showUnshelved` (toggles workspace setting `gittree.shelf.showUnshelved`, consumed by BOTH surfaces). **New store state:** `ShelfMeta.unshelvedAt` + `markUnshelved()` — set when a clean full unshelve keeps the entry, absent on pre-T6 entries (backward compatible); marked entries hide behind the toggle and render greyed/check-iconed in both surfaces. **Integrity inline, never modal:** the bridge ships `valid` (= `store.verify`) per entry on every push, damaged rows get a warning icon + `damaged` text in tab and tree, and the engine's `readPatch` sha256 gate refuses the unshelve (error lands in the window's banner — moved OUT of the Commit TabPanel to window level, because a failed shelf action was invisible from the Shelf tab; caught by the new Playwright test). The `gittree.shelf` tree view is now gated `when: gittree.commitWindow == native` per plan step 3. Fixed in passing: switching to the Shelf tab unmounts the Commit panel, and the message editor remounted with its mount-time `initial` — stale text after a tab round trip; the editor now seeds from the live mirror (safe: `initial` never touches the DOM after mount, and mid-composition the mirror is frozen). Testing: +7 Playwright (strip + entries/files/binary flags; damaged renders inline with warning icon; eye toggle reveals/hides applied entries; toolbar dispatches `commit/shelfAction` as a request with entry identity and the list updates; rows carry `webviewSection`/`shelfId`/`relPath` context for the native menus; file activate posts openDiff; a refused action surfaces in the banner), +5 integration (command-set parity — every tree action has a dispatchable command; bridge state mirrors a real shelve and its dispatched Unshelve Silently applies the patch, removes the entry and lands files in the named changelist — the same-state acceptance by construction; a tampered `changes.patch` reads `valid:false`, refuses to touch worktree or entry; unshelve-keeping sets `unshelvedAt`, hides by default, `gittree.shelf.showUnshelved` reveals with `unshelved:true`; Compare-with-Local opens the `Shelved: … ↔ Local`-titled diff tab). docs/settings.md regenerated. Gates: typecheck+lint clean, 480 unit, 89 webview (+7), 129 integration passing + 2 pending; only red in the full run was the documented stash-500ms load flake.

P11-T7 ✅ 2026-08-23 — Shelve Changes dialog with embedded diff (gap-analysis S14 ticked). First `dialogHost` consumer: new vite entry `dialogs` (`webview-ui/src/dialogs/{main,ShelveChanges,dialogs.css}`, routed by the initial-state `dialog` id), and `DialogOptions.handlers` so a dialog can serve lazy data (`shelveDialog/fileDiff`, `shelveDialog/refresh`) beyond the one-shot init payload — the P13 dialogs will need the same. §2A layout: toolbar (expand/collapse/refresh/preview) → `Changelist: ▾` selector (changelists with files, plus Unversioned Files) → checkbox FileTree with per-file `n of m changes` badges → `N of M modified` → message (prefilled from the commit-window draft, else the list name; first line = shelf name, rest = comment) → collapsible `∨ Diff` (InlineDiff + per-change checkboxes; collapsed state persists via webview state) → `[Shelve Changes] [Cancel]` + footer `[x] Remove shelved changes from the worktree`. Per-change semantics mirror P11-T3 exactly: all-covered = no selection, excluding the last change unticks the file, selections carry signatures the host re-checks at accept — stale falls back to whole-file with a warning. **Two plan mechanisms replaced (deviations recorded):** (1) step 2's `buildPatch` body emits zero-context hunks the unshelve ladder can't consume; `ShelfStore.shelveSelected` instead assembles the shelved state in a scratch index (read-tree HEAD → add whole files → hash-object+update-index the partial included-texts with HEAD's mode) and lets `git diff --cached HEAD --binary --full-index` write a completely ordinary patch. (2) step 3's reverse-apply of a complement patch fails on adjacent hunks (context overlap); removal instead writes the `applyLineChanges`-computed residue (HEAD+excluded, byte-exact CR intact) directly, with verify-then-remove scoped to the whole-file sections. **Fix discovered by the round-trip test:** `git apply --3way` implies `--index` and refuses with `does not match index` when index ≠ worktree — so the residue is left *staged* (index == worktree), letting the ladder fall through to the recorded-blob merge; `unstageAfterApply` returns it after unshelve, and changelists read staged/unstaged alike. **Shipped-bug fix:** the commit window's Shelve toolbar button executed `gittree.shelve` — a command id that never existed, so the button errored since T2; it now opens the dialog (`gittree.shelve.dialog`, also invocable from a changelist node or the palette). Accept-path extracted as `applyShelveResult` so integration drives the exact webview payload. Testing: +8 Playwright (`dialogs.spec.ts` over a router-faithful mock: §2A shape with prefill/collapsed-diff/remove-checked; changelist switch resets ticks and lists unversioned; cancel + Esc post `dialog/cancel`; whole-file accept payload with paths/message/remove flag; per-change exclusion lands in `partials` with signature and badges the row; excluding all unticks and drops the file; whole-file-only files say so; Shelve disables without message or ticks), +4 integration (1-of-3-changes shelve: stored entry named from the subject with the comment, worktree keeps the other two byte-identically, and unshelving onto the residue merges the change back via the 3way ladder — apply --index fails on context by construction; CRLF round trip with zero EOL damage; stale signature shelves whole; empty result is a provable no-op). Gates: typecheck+lint clean, 480 unit, 97 webview (+8), 133 integration passing + 2 pending; only red in the full run was the stash-500ms load flake (502 ms). Extension + webview production builds verified with the new entry.

P11-T8 ✅ 2026-08-23 — Density: counts, status colors, directory highlighting. `N files` badges were already on every group and folder node since T2 (S12); this task lands the palette. New `webview-ui/src/lib/status.ts` (pure, root-suite unit-tested): status→codicon and status→color where the defaults are the theme's own `gitDecoration-*` variables (with a `--vscode-foreground` fallback so exotic themes never render blank), and `gittree.colors.<status>` overrides win when set; `dirStatusColor` gives IntelliJ's directory highlighting — a folder whose descendants share one status wears it, mixed folders read as plain modified. New `src/ui/statusColors.ts` reads the six new `gittree.colors.*` settings and validates them (`#hex`/CSS keyword only — the value lands in a style attribute, so nothing else passes); shipped as `CommitStateDto.colors` + `ShelveDialogParamsDto.colors`, and a `gittree.colors` config change repaints via a state push without reload. `FileTree` gains `nameColor` — IntelliJ colors the *names*, not only the icons — and the commit tree, Shelf tab and Shelve dialog all render status-colored names, status-colored file icons, and status-colored folder icons+names from a new `dirKinds` rollup in `assembleCommitTree` (per-group bottom-up walk). Deviations recorded: overrides apply to GitTree's own webview surfaces (VS Code has no per-extension decoration-color override for native trees — same theme tokens are the webview defaults, so they agree unless overridden), and no second color-blind palette for status colors (theme + overrides already cover it; the P10-T2 graph palette is untouched). Testing: +5 unit (defaults with fallback, override precedence, empty-override fallthrough, icon table, dir rollup single/mixed/empty/override), +3 Playwright (override colors icon+name while untracked keeps its theme variable; a mixed directory reads modified with the colored folder icon; a single-status group root wears that status), +2 integration (overrides ship with the state and an injection-shaped value is dropped; counts/kinds/origPath stay correct across modify + git mv + delete + untracked, and the rename source never appears as a second row). docs/settings.md regenerated. Gates: typecheck+lint clean, 485 unit, 100 webview, 134 integration passing + 2 pending; the two reds in the full run (phase05 recordOrigin — a transient Windows HEAD-lock on `git checkout -f`; the stash-500ms budget) both pass in isolation.

P11-T9 ✅ 2026-08-23 — Docs, budgets, migration — Phase 11 CLOSED, every parity-checklist item ticked. FEATURES.md §3 rewritten from the §2A shapes: every item now carries a SHAPE criterion (which container, which toolbar, which menu) beside its behaviour — the missing ingredient the gap analysis §1 blamed for the old audit passing while the product looked nothing like IntelliJ. Sections: the window itself (one panel, first in SCM, tabs/toolbar/tree/bottom block, the `native` escape hatch), the change tree (folder rows, tri-state, unversioned, per-change inclusion, density), both paradigms, the message editor (undo/IME/history/amend ▾), the gear popup (Git / Commit Checks / Advanced with the defer caption), Shelf tab + Shelve dialog, commit actions. docs/deviations.md: D3 gains the P11 webview-menu note (kit `Menu` for interpolated labels, native `webview/context` for static ones, native menus for tree views); D7 was already updated at T5. Walkthrough gains a `commitWindow` step (media reused, completion `onView:gittree.commit`) telling users how to get the old surface back; CHANGELOG.md gains the full Unreleased section — the window, per-change commits, editor, gear, shelf, dialog, colors, the deferAdvanced behaviour change, and the three shipped-bug fixes (inert intents, phantom shelve command id, zero-height tree) — which is what the what's-new notification opens on the next minor bump. Budgets re-checked with the commit+dialogs entries: VSIX 1.21 MB (1.14 MB effective sans images) vs the 2.5 MB budget; `npm run test:perf` all six §13 budgets green (activation sync, status p95 @10k, log first page @100k, blame cold/cached, log-session memory) and the Playwright 60fps scroll spec green in the webview suite. Gates: typecheck+lint clean, 485 unit, 100 webview, 135 integration passing + 2 pending; the only red in the full run was the stash-500ms load flake (passes in isolation — the phase05 transient from the T8 run also passed again). Phase 11 total: 8 commits, S1/S11/S11b/S12/S13/S14 all closed.

P12-T1 ✅ 2026-08-23 — Git tool-window shell: tabs + four-pane layout. The single-pane log webview is restructured into the §2A shape: `Log` tab (Tabs kit; `Console` arrives with T8 — dead-tab rule, the P11-T2 precedent) → filter bar → new `Shell.tsx` with nested SplitPanes `[Branches | Commits] | [Changed Files / Commit Details]` — the right column is the vertical split, every ratio persisted (`outer`/`left`/`right`), double-click resets to defaults (P11-T1 SplitPane). Each pane owns a **header row**: chevron + title + a per-pane toolbar slot, so pane actions stop competing for the single view/title strip — the Changed Files header already hosts the `N files` count, multi-diff and flat/tree toggles that used to crowd the details panel. **Panes collapse to slim bars** (vertical for Branches, horizontal for the right column), IntelliJ-style, and keep their state because they only hide; the Commits pane deliberately never collapses (it is the window's centre, and folding it would unmount the sized graph container). **Keyboard rail:** every header is focusable with a `:focus-visible` ring and an aria pane name; `Tab`/`Shift+Tab` cycle the four headers, Enter/ArrowDown drops into the pane body. **One selection model, host-side:** new `log/select` request mirrors the webview's multi-select into `LogViewProvider.selectedCommits`, which "Compare"/"Squash"/"Push all up to here" (T7) will read instead of racing copies; `hasHandler` seam added (the P11-T3 pattern) and a new phase12 integration suite asserts every webview→host log method is answered. `DetailsPanel` slims to metadata + refs + body; the changed-file list moves to `ChangedFilesPane.tsx` (T1 stub over `details.files`, status-colored via the P11-T8 palette; T3 rewrites it on `git show --name-status`). Persisted webview state is now merge-written (`persist()`), because the old two writers clobbered each other's keys — layout, filter and focus share one bag. Testing: +5 Playwright (four named panes + aria labels + centre-pane-no-collapse; Tab/Shift+Tab cycling; collapse folds to a bar while the sibling keeps working and state survives re-expand; ratios/collapse survive a reload via the now-sessionStorage-backed mock state; the selection mirrors to the host as one model), 1 updated (details assertions split across the two right-column panes), chip-picker test made `exact` (the new pane headers substring-matched 'Branch'); +2 integration (handler contract over LOG_METHODS; selection model default). The 100k-commit 60 fps scroll spec still passes with four panes mounted. Gates: typecheck+lint clean, 485 unit, 105 webview, 137 integration passing + 2 pending; only red in the full run was the stash-500ms load flake.

P12-T2 ✅ 2026-08-23 — Branches pane + vertical icon rail (gap-analysis S2b ticked). New `webview-ui/src/log/BranchesPane.tsx`: `HEAD (Current)` → `Local` (favorites flat-first with a star — IntelliJ pins them — then treeModel **folder nodes** for the rest, so `feature/f1`, `f1-1`, `f2` collapse under one `feature` row) → `Remote` per remote (short names under each remote dir) → `Tags`; the §2A **vertical icon rail** on the pane's left edge: `+` new branch · `−` delete · `<>` compare-with-current · `Q` filter/search (webview-local flat filter) · `★` favorites-only (webview-local) · `@` show-my-commits (round-trips through the author filter using host-served `user.email`) · `>` collapse pane — every one an aria-labelled button. **Selecting refs filters the Commits pane** through the existing filter model (`refs: [names]`; additive with Ctrl; `HEAD (Current)` restores the default). New shared registry `src/branches/branchActions.ts` (checkout / newFrom / compareWithCurrent / diffWithWorkingTree / rebaseOntoCurrent / push / rename / delete / toggleFavorite, each with interpolated labels + applicability) consumed by the new `src/log/branchesPaneBridge.ts` — the P13-T5 popup will read the same list, so the surfaces cannot drift; the pane's **native context menu** (`webview/context`, static labels) dispatches `gittree.log.branch.*` commands that resolve the repo by **canonical key with no fallback** (acting on some other repo because the path spelling differed — 8.3 short names — would be worse than not acting; caught by an integration test that passed the short form). Host: `log/branches` (grouped DTO with favorites/current/detached + cached `user.email`), `log/branchAction`, and `log/branchesChanged` pushed on model refs changes AND favorite toggles (payload-free — the webview's re-request dedups bursts). **Design decision hardened by a failing test:** `applies()` gates menu *enumeration* only, never dispatch — the bridge initially refused actions when the (stale) snapshot said they didn't apply, and a checkout dispatched right after another checkout silently no-op'd because the refresher hadn't caught up; dispatch now always runs, and an invalid action gets git's own error. Testing: +4 Playwright (sections + one-`feature`-folder + favorite-starred-and-first + remote/tag counts; ref click posts `refs:['feature/f1']`, Ctrl adds `f2`, HEAD restores the preset; all seven rail buttons accessible by name and `+` posts the registry's `new`; branch rows carry `{webviewSection:'logBranch', refName, refType}` and `★`/`@` toggles work), +3 integration (DTO groups refs/favorites/current from a real repo; registry dispatch and the native-menu command run the same checkout both ways — the command deliberately fed the 8.3 path; `setFilter refs:['feature/f2']` pages the REAL webview until rowCount equals `rev-list --count`, anchored to repoC via an opened editor because the session follows the active repo). Existing log specs re-scoped (`commitRow` helper) after the second virtual tree entered the page. Gates: typecheck+lint clean, 485 unit, 109 webview, 140 integration passing + 2 pending; only red in the full run was the stash-500ms load flake.

P12-T3 ✅ 2026-08-23 — Changed Files pane. Rewritten as a real `FileTree` over the selection's change set: the §2A repo row (`repo · N files · <dim repo path>`, repo icon) → folder rows with `N files` counts → status-colored file names/icons (P11-T8 palette, shipped to the log via `LogInitDto.colors`) with rename pairs (`← old/path`) and directory status rollup. **Multi-commit selection shows the aggregate** the way IntelliJ's "Compare Versions" reads: new `LogService.getAggregateFiles(oldest, newest)` runs `git diff <oldest>^..<newest> --name-status -z -M` (root commit → the empty-tree hash stands in), the host orders the selection by graph position, and the DTO carries `base`/`newest` so a file click opens the **range-scoped** diff — `log/openFileDiff` accepts an optional `base` and titles the editor `old..new`. Pane toolbar: `N files` count · multi-diff (single-commit semantics, disabled on a range) · tree/flat toggle (full paths under the repo row in flat mode) · **preview-on-click** toggle (single click opens the diff preview; persisted) · **Show History for Revision** (enabled on a selected file row; runs `gittree.showFileHistory` for that file through the new `log/showFileHistory`). Deviation: `by-module` grouping is omitted — IntelliJ modules have no VS Code analogue and the directory tree is the honest grouping (recorded in the phase doc). Testing: +3 Playwright (repo row + folder counts + theme-variable status colors + flat mode round trip; a 2-commit selection posts `changedFiles {shas:[2]}`, renders the aggregate fixture, disables multi-diff and dblclick posts `openFileDiff` with the range base; preview-on-click opens on single click and Show History posts the file path), +3 integration (rename commit yields exactly one `R` entry with the orig path and a clean merge matches `git show --name-status`'s combined-diff emptiness — matching git IS the acceptance; a 500-file commit reports all 500 `A` entries; the 3-commit aggregate equals `git diff <oldest>^..<newest>` path-for-path and a root-commit aggregate falls back to the empty tree). Gates: typecheck+lint clean, 485 unit, 112 webview, 143 integration passing + 2 pending; only red in the full run was the stash-500ms load flake.

P12-T4 ✅ 2026-08-23 — Commit Details pane rewritten to the §2A layout: subject line → `<hash> <author>` (+ Copy Revision Number and new **Copy Subject**) → `<email> on <date> at <time>` → ref chips typed by kind (HEAD/branch/`tag:` with the existing palette). What the old panel lacked, added: **committer line** when it differs from the author; **GPG signature state** — `%G?` joined the `getCommitDetails` format (costs nothing without gpg), shipped as `CommitDetailsDto.signature`, rendered `Good signature`/`BAD signature`/`unverified`/`cannot be checked` and *nothing* for `N`; **clickable parent links** (`parent abc1234` → new `log/goTo` → the existing `revealCommit` pages until found and the reveal event moves the graph selection); **`Contained in branches: Show all`** — deliberately lazy: new `LogService.getContainingBranches` (`git branch --contains --format=%(refname:short)`) runs on click only, with a `containsCalls` counter as the laziness seam the acceptance names. **Body linkification**: new pure `linkify.ts` — URLs always, issue keys when `gittree.log.issuePattern` + `gittree.log.issueUrl` are set (`$0` = the match; invalid pattern disables issues but keeps URLs; URL wins on overlap); config changes re-init the view; body remains selectable text. Testing: +11 unit (linkify URL/issue/overlap/invalid-pattern/empty; signatureLabel mapping table), +5 Playwright (subject/meta/committer/signature/tag-chip for the signed fixture and NO signature row for `N`; Copy Subject copies the subject not the sha; a parent click posts goTo and the reveal selects the parent row; contained-in stays at 0 host calls until the click then chips render; body anchors carry the URL and templated issue hrefs), +2 integration (an unsigned commit reads `N` — never blank — with parents present; `--contains` runs zero times through details and once on demand, answering the current branch). docs/settings.md regenerated. Gates: typecheck+lint clean, 491 unit, 117 webview, 145 integration passing + 2 pending; only red in the full run was the stash-500ms load flake.

P12-T5 ✅ 2026-08-23 — Filter row and toolbar parity. `FilterBar` rewritten to the §2A shape: a `Branch or tag` combo (datalist over every local/remote/tag; Enter applies `refs:[v]`, `HEAD`/`ALL` presets included) · `Text or hash` (a hash-shaped Enter navigates via the new Go-to path instead of grepping) with the existing `.*`/`Cc` toggles · four REAL **multi-select dropdowns with search** replacing the host QuickPick chips: `Branch ▾` (locals favorites-first with stars, remotes, tags, All-branches; live checkbox toggles), `User ▾` (`Me (user.email)` + the author list served by new `log/authors` over `LogService.getAuthors`, searchable, capped render), `Date ▾` (Today/Yesterday/7/30 presets + native from/until date inputs), `Paths ▾` (chips with ×, free-text add — multiple compose — and `Browse…` still delegating to the host's real file picker). Right-side toolbar: refresh (`log/refresh`) · **Go to Hash/Branch/Tag** (`log/goToRef` — host InputBox when no ref passed, `rev-parse --verify ^{commit}` then `revealCommit`) · **Show Details** (folds/unfolds the whole right column via the T1 layout) · **Highlight my commits** (webview-local `gt-mine` row highlight matched on `user.name`, shipped as `LogBranchesDto.myName`) · **Saved filters ▾** (kit Menu: apply preset / Save current filter… / Delete preset ›; presets persist in the new `gittree.log.filterPresets` workspace setting and ship in `LogInitDto.presets`, which is what makes them survive reloads). The refs snapshot is now fetched once in App and shared by the Branches pane and the filter row. **Omitted, not faked (deviation):** `Collapse linear branches` and `Show long edges` — the §13 graph model is row-local by design and neither is expressible without whole-graph span knowledge; a budgeted GraphLayout rework, not a toolbar toggle. Testing: +2 unit (the remaining regex/match-case combos — all four now covered; the 2-path argv order was already pinned), +6 Playwright (Branch ▾ search + favorites-first + star + live toggle posting `refs:['feature/f1']`; User ▾ Me + searchable authors; Date preset posts today's `--since`; Paths two-entry composition + Browse delegation; hash-Enter and the toolbar both hit goToRef; saved preset applies, Save posts, Show Details folds both right panes; Highlight-mine marks Alice's rows only), +2 integration (a preset survives in settings and its filter restores through a session reset; a two-path filter pages to exactly `git rev-list --count HEAD -- a b` through the real webview). docs/settings.md regenerated. Gates: typecheck+lint clean, 491 unit, 122 webview, **148 integration passing + 2 pending — fully green run** (even the stash-500ms budget passed this time).

P12-T6 ✅ 2026-08-23 — Patch engine: Create Patch / Apply Patch (§3.9 was absent entirely — no format-patch or am anywhere in src/). New `src/rewrite/patch/createPatch.ts`: commits (any selection order — `rev-list --topo-order --no-walk` orders them oldest→newest so the mailbox replays in history order) and ranges via `git format-patch --binary --full-index`, emitted **one file per commit into a scratch dir and concatenated with fs** — executor stdout is utf8-decoded and would corrupt binary payloads (the P7-T3 lesson reapplied); worktree selections via the scratch-index technique (`read-tree HEAD` → `add` → `hash-object`+`update-index` → `diff --cached HEAD --output=`) because the plan's `buildPatch` emits zero-context hunks only `apply --unidiff-zero` reads (deviation recorded). `createPatchText` is a latin1 (byte-preserving) read for clipboard targets. New `src/rewrite/patch/applyPatch.ts`: mailbox detection (`From <40-hex>` line anchor), `git am -3` replays mailboxes **as commits**; an am that cannot complete is `--abort`ed — never left paused — and the same file degrades to a content-only `git apply --3way` (+`--reverse`/`-p<n>`/`--whitespace=fix` options for the P13-T4 dialog), whose conflicts land as ordinary unmerged entries the P6 conflict flow already owns; `--reject` is never passed so `.rej` litter cannot exist; failures report git's own per-file errors. `summarizePatch` (over `patchParser.splitPatchFiles`) gives the dialog its files+hunk-counts pre-apply summary. Command `gittree.patch.apply` registered (file picker, env-seeded for tests; reports applied-as-commits / conflicts-with-files / failure-with-reason). Testing: +2 unit (mailbox detection incl. a body that merely mentions "From cafe"; the pre-apply summary shape), +4 integration (the acceptance verbatim: 2-commit patch → `reset --hard` → apply replays via am to an **identical tree hash** with subjects intact; a deliberately conflicting patch reports exactly its file with zero `.rej` anywhere; a binary blob (NULs, 0xFF…) round-trips byte-exactly through `--binary` + am; a worktree-selection patch carries real context lines and applies back cleanly). Gates: typecheck+lint clean, 493 unit, 122 webview (unchanged — no webview code this task), 151 integration passing + 2 pending; only red in the full run was the stash-500ms load flake.

P12-T7 ✅ 2026-08-23 — Commit context menu completion. The reference menu's missing items, all real: **Create Patch…** (T6 engine, multi-select aware, save-dialog with env seam) · **Fixup…/Squash Into…** — refuse with a reason when the target is not an ancestor of HEAD, commit the ticked changelist paths with `--fixup`/`--squash`, then offer a **non-interactive autosquash** (`rebase -i --autosquash --autostash <target>^` with `GIT_SEQUENCE_EDITOR=:`/`GIT_EDITOR=:` — git runs editors through its own sh, Windows included; a conflicted run pauses into the P5 monitor + P6 conflict flow, snapshot taken first) · **Push All up to Here** — `git push <remote> <sha>:refs/heads/<branch>` with not-on-current-branch and non-fast-forward both refused BEFORE invoking (no force fallback — that is P5 push-safety's job) · **Branches ›** — lazy `--contains` (branches + tags) as a QuickPick chain (native webview menus cannot nest dynamic content, D3), each ref offering checkout/new-branch-from/compare through the shared `branchActions` registry · **Go to Parent** (`rev-list --parents`, merge commits pick) / **Go to Child** (nearest LOADED row listing the sha as parent — `findLoadedChild` walks bottom-up) · **Show Repository at Revision** — new `gittree-tree:` **FileSystemProvider** (`src/core/vdocs/revTreeFs.ts`): read-only stat/readDirectory/readFile over `ls-tree -z --long` + `git unpack-file` (git writes blob bytes itself — binary-safe), every mutation refused, plus the dedicated `gittree.revTree` view (`when: gittree.revTree.active`, close button) whose file rows open through the provider read-only — deviation D8 honoured (no second project frame exists). **`Reset → Keep`** joins Soft/Mixed/Hard (P12-T7.6): destructive-flagged with a P9 snapshot like Hard, and a refusal (git protecting local changes) surfaces git's own reason instead of a stack trace. Menu contributions on the commit rows (nav/patch/rewrite/push/refs groups), palette-hidden. Testing: +6 integration (fixup+autosquash folds the ticked change into the target with LINEAR history — no `fixup!` subjects survive — and the rewritten target contains the folded content; a non-ancestor fixup refuses changing nothing; Push-All-up-to-Here against a **local bare remote** pushes exactly the selected commit — HEAD provably NOT pushed — and a non-FF retry leaves the remote untouched; `gittree-tree:` serves a 3-commits-ago file byte-equal to `git show`, stats Readonly, refuses writes, opens in an editor, and the view target round-trips through show/close; Reset-Keep refuses while a conflicting local change exists — HEAD and the change both intact — then succeeds on a clean file). Test-fix on the way: the suite initially passed `head~1` (not sha-shaped) which `shaOf` silently rejects — a false-positive pattern worth remembering. Gates: typecheck+lint clean, 493 unit, 122 webview (no webview changes), 156 integration passing + 2 pending; only red in the full run was the stash-500ms load flake.

P12-T8 ✅ 2026-08-23 — Console tab (gap-analysis S10 ticked). New `src/core/git/commandLog.ts`: a 2 000-entry ring of every executor run — seq, timestamp, argv, exit code, duration, stderr+stdout head (2 000 chars) — fed by a new structured `onDidRun` hook on the executor's existing trace path (the `GitTree` output channel is untouched; this is a mirror, not a replacement). **Redaction happens at the ring's front door**, so no secret can exist in the buffer, cross RPC, or reach the DOM: URL userinfo (`https://user:token@host` → `https://***@host`), askpass env/handshake values, generic `token/password/secret=` values, and `--author=` identities are scrubbed from argv and output before `push` stores anything; unit tests pin the acceptance's tokenized-remote-URL and askpass cases. Ring size honours the phase's `gittree.log.console.limit` setting (default 2 000, min 100, read once at activation — live-resizing a ring has churn for no one). Webview: the tool window's tab strip finally earns its second tab — `Log | Console` (the T1 dead-tab rule resolved), active tab persisted. New `ConsolePane.tsx`: one collapsible row per command (time · `git argv` · `→ exit` · duration), **failures pre-expanded and coloured but still collapsible** — first cut's `open = failed || toggled` made a failed row's chevron a silent no-op, shipped as XOR (`failed !== toggled`) instead — plus a client-side filter box, per-row Copy command / Copy output (through the existing `log/copy` host path), and Clear (host `log/consoleClear` → `CommandLog.clear`, also a palette command `gittree.log.console.clear`). Delta protocol: `log/console {afterSeq}` serves increments; the 200 ms-throttled `log/consoleChanged` ping makes the pane refetch the FULL capped ring rather than a delta, because a host-side clear resets seqs below the webview's cursor. DOM mirrors the 2 000 cap so memory is bounded on both sides. **Landmine defused:** `TabPanel` unmounts the inactive tab, so switching Log → Console → Log remounted the commits pane with its old object-ref ResizeObserver still bound to the detached node (the exact P11-T4 lesson resurfacing) — the graph container now measures through the callback-ref `useElementSize`. Testing: +10 unit (redactText URL/askpass/token/author + leave-plain-text-alone; redactArgv wholesale `--author=`; ring seq/delta/redact-before-store/stderr-first join/2 000-char head/2 000-entry cap with oldest dropped/custom limit + clear + change events), +5 Playwright (three-run fixture renders with the failing fetch pre-expanded, coloured, host-redacted URL visible and exit 128; chevrons expand a success and collapse a failure both ways; filter narrows to the fetch and Copy command posts the redacted argv; Clear posts consoleClear and empties; the active tab survives a reload and the Log tab's rows remount intact), +2 integration (a successful and a failing `rev-parse` through the core executor both land in the ring with exit codes and timing intact; `entriesAfter` serves strict deltas and the `gittree.log.console.clear` command empties the ring; the T1 handler-contract test auto-covers the two new LOG_METHODS). docs/settings.md regenerated. Gates: typecheck+lint clean, 503 unit, 127 webview, 158 integration passing + 2 pending; the only red in the full run was a NEW environmental flake — P5’s `git checkout -f main` hit “unable to write symref for HEAD: Permission denied” despite gitWithRetry’s 10 tries (~22 s) under full-suite load, and the P5 suite passes 19/19 in isolation.

P12-T9 ✅ 2026-08-23 — Perf re-validation and docs — **PHASE 12 CLOSED**. `test:perf` re-run with the four-pane window shipped: all §13 budgets hold — activation 6.1 ms (≤100), status p95 230 ms (≤400), blame 80.8/0 ms (≤300/30), memory +64.4 MB (≤150); **log first page 611.5 ms** vs the 600 ms row is within the suite's documented 15% tolerance with git's own streaming at 590.5 ms of it (2026-07-16 read 542.1 — overnight suite load; noted in perf.md rather than papered over, budget NOT touched). The Playwright frame-time spec — which has run against the full four-pane page since T1 — reads median 16.8 ms / p95 18.2 ms at 100k commits. Two new **recorded** benches follow the suite's no-§13-row convention (trend, not assertion): selection → Details + Changed Files populated concurrently on a cold service (81.5 ms median — pane population is lazy by construction, ids pushed, each pane fetches its own data) and Changed Files @ a **5 000-file commit** (80.1 ms, all 5 000 reported; the wide commit now caps the perf log fixture at HEAD in runPerf.ts). Bundle budget re-checked via `npm run package`: 1.15 MB effective vs 2.50 MB. Docs: `docs/perf.md` gains the 2026-08-23 four-pane results table + notes (lazy pane population, the Console ring's bounded-by-construction memory, frame spec runs four-pane) above the kept 2026-07-16 history; **FEATURES.md §4 rewritten in the §3 shape-criteria style** — the window itself (bottom-docked `gittree-panel` webview, `Log | Console` tabs, four panes in nested persisted splitters, host-side selection model), Branches pane + rail, graph + the §2A filter row, both right panes, the Console tab, and the COMPLETE commit context menu as shipped (interactive rebase, undo/revert/cherry-pick/drop/reword, patches, fixup/squash+autosquash, push-all-up-to-here, reset incl. Keep, branches ›, parent/child, show-repo-at-revision), with IntelliSort and no-re-run-console named as deviations instead of implied. Phase-12 parity checklist fully ticked (S2 closed by T1–T4 + this re-validation, S10 by T8). Gates: typecheck+lint clean, 503 unit, 127 webview, 159 integration passing + 2 pending in a FULLY GREEN full run (even the two known flakes stayed quiet), test:perf 8/8, bundle check green.

P13-T1 ✅ 2026-08-23 — **Push dialog** (gap analysis S4, §2A "Push dialog"), the first real option-bearing dialog replacing a QuickPick chain. **Shape:** title `Push Commits to <repo>`; left pane one section per repository, each headed by the editable `<local> → <remote> : <remote-branch>` arrow row (clicking it swaps in a remote `<select>` + a branch field with a `<datalist>` of that remote's branches; Enter or `Apply` recomputes host-side) over the outgoing commit list (sha · subject · author · date, capped at 100 with an "… and N more" row); right pane the push's **full changed-file tree** — `N files` header, folder counts, P11-T8 status colors — with its own **right-side vertical toolbar** (Show Diff · Preview on Click · Edit Source · Group by Directory · expand/collapse), the two file actions reaching the host over `pushDialog/openDiff` (through the P1 `gittree-rev:` provider, with the add/delete one-sided cases) and `pushDialog/openFile`. Footer per §2A: `?` · `[ ] Push tags: [All | Current Branch]` · `[x] Run Git hooks` · `[Push ▾]` split button (`Force Push…`) · `[Cancel]`, plus the dim **"will run:"** line. **Argv** is one vscode-free module (`src/scm/dialogs/pushArgs.ts`) that the webview and the host *both* import, so the previewed command is literally the executed command: `--no-verify` (hooks off), `--tags`/`--follow-tags`, `--force-with-lease` (+`--force-if-includes` behind `core.git.caps`, never bare `--force`), `--set-upstream` only when publishing under the branch's own name, and always an explicit `<remote> <local>:refs/heads/<remoteBranch>` refspec — which is what makes retargeting honest. **Outgoing model:** tracking ref present → `<tracking>..HEAD` + `diff <tracking> HEAD`; absent → `HEAD --not --remotes=<remote>` (what git would actually transfer) with the diff base falling back to the span's boundary parent, then the empty tree for a root-spanning push (the P12-T3 rule). Force keeps the P5 confirmation, now **naming the exact commands** in its detail. New: `gittree.push.dialog`, settings `gittree.push.runHooks` / `gittree.push.tags` / `gittree.dialogs.showCommandLine` under a new "GitTree: Dialogs" section, `registerPhase13Dialogs` as Phase 13's own entry point in `extension.ts`. `kindOf` moved from `ChangedFilesPane` into `lib/status.ts` so the dialog does not pull the log app in. Tests: **+19 unit** (the whole flag matrix incl. tags-with-force and publish-branch, `shouldSetUpstream`, `splitUpstream`'s longest-known-remote rule, command formatting), **+10 Playwright** (§2A shape, the will-run line tracking every option, accept payloads, the split button's Force path, retarget adopt-the-recomputed-section, group-by, the three toolbar actions), **+9 integration** against a **local bare file remote** (handler-surface contract; the section defaults to the sole remote; publish sets upstream; the file tree equals `git diff --name-status <remote>/<branch>..HEAD`; retarget pushes to the chosen ref *without* repointing tracking; a rejecting `pre-push` blocks the push and `Run Git hooks` off skips it with `--no-verify` in argv; force declined never reaches git and accepted carries `--force-with-lease` only; an unknown root is reported, never redirected). docs/settings.md regenerated. Gates: typecheck+lint clean, 522 unit, 137 webview + 3 skipped, 167 integration passing + 2 pending — the single red is the pre-existing P7-T2 500 ms stash-view bound, which I proved independent of this task by disabling `registerPhase13Dialogs` behind a temporary env seam: **P13 off → 3/3 failures (537/515/539 ms), P13 on → 2/3 (522/527 ms)**. Budget NOT loosened; seam removed.

P13-T2 ✅ 2026-08-23 — **Update Project dialog** (gap analysis S4, §2A "Update Project dialog"). **Shape:** the radio pair `(o) Merge incoming changes into the current branch` / `( ) Rebase the current branch on top of incoming changes` over a `<branch> ← <upstream> · N behind · M ahead` summary, `[ ] Do not show again` (which explains itself inline — "Ctrl+T will run Merge directly. Restore the dialog with 'GitTree: Update Project Strategy…'"), footer `[Reset to the Remote Branch] · [OK] [Cancel]`, and the dim "will run" line showing **both** the OK plan and the reset plan. **Execution is fetch-then-integrate, not `git pull`** — two deliberate reasons, both in `updateArgs.ts`'s header: (1) the radio must be authoritative, and `git pull` obeys `pull.rebase`, so a user who picked Merge could silently get a rebase; an explicit `merge <upstream>` / `rebase <upstream>` cannot be overridden; (2) the fetch boundary is measurable — holding the **upstream** sha before and after the fetch is what lets the result report the true incoming volume for a rebase as well as a merge, where HEAD before→after would over-count rewritten local commits. Merge is plain (no `--no-ff`, so an undiverged branch fast-forwards; `--no-ff` belongs to T3's Merge dialog). **`Do not show again`** persists to the new `gittree.update.strategy` setting (`ask`|`merge`|`rebase`) and `showUpdateDialog` then bypasses the webview entirely; the new `gittree.update.configure` command ("GitTree: Update Project Strategy…") is the gear that restores `ask`. **`Reset to the Remote Branch`** = fetch + `reset --hard <upstream>` behind an explicit destructive confirmation, enqueued `isDestructive` with `SNAPSHOT_LABELS.reset` so P9 snapshots the dirty worktree first — an integration test proves the discarded uncommitted edit is in the snapshot and therefore recoverable. **Result reporting** (deviation D9's replacement for the `Update Info` tab): a notification with commits pulled / files changed and a `Show in Log` action that applies a `{range: {a: upstreamBefore, b: upstreamAfter}}` filter through `gittree.log.setFilter` (which focuses the log first). Keybindings repointed to the dialogs, matching IntelliJ: **`Ctrl+T` → `gittree.update.dialog`** and **`Ctrl+Shift+K` → `gittree.push.dialog`** (both still gated on `gittree.keymap.intellij`; the silent `gittree.pull`/`gittree.push` remain); docs/keyboard.md updated. `formatGitCommand` moved to a shared `commandLine.ts` (with `formatGitCommands` for multi-step plans) now that two dialogs preview plans. Tests: **+10 unit** (strategy normalization incl. the "anything unknown is ask" rule, fetch/integrate/reset argv, the full plan for both actions, and that neither strategy ever emits `pull`), **+6 Playwright** (§2A shape, the will-run line following the radio while reset stays fixed, OK payload, the `Do not show again` note and payload, and that **`Reset to the Remote Branch` never records a strategy** even with the box ticked), **+6 integration** (params carry branch/upstream/divergence and default to merge; merge fast-forwards and reports 1 commit / 1 file with the range resolving to the real remote sha; rebase rewrites the local commit onto the incoming one with a clean tree and no merge commit; a remembered strategy runs Ctrl+T directly with **no dialog tab opened** and the gear restores `ask`; reset is declined-safe and, when accepted, lands on the upstream with a recoverable `Before: reset` snapshot; a branch with no upstream reports instead of running). **Two harness defects found and fixed while getting the gate green:** (1) `reportUpdate` *awaited* a notification carrying an action button — those stay pending until dismissed, so `showUpdateDialog` hung forever in tests and would have hung on the user's dismissal in real use; it is now fire-and-forget with the choice handled in `.then`. (2) `phase07`/`phase09` **`delete`d `GITTREE_TEST_AUTOCONFIRM`**, which the runner sets once for the whole process — silently disarming auto-confirm for every suite that ran afterwards (it broke my reset test the first time the two met). New shared `withAutoConfirm()` helper in `test/integration/setup.ts` saves and restores; all four old sites and both P13 sites now use it. Gates: typecheck+lint clean, 532 unit, 143 webview + 3 skipped, **173 integration passing + 2 pending**, the only red being the pre-existing P7-T2 500 ms stash bound already flagged in the nightlog.

P13-T3 ✅ 2026-08-23 — **Merge · Rebase · Reset · Rollback dialogs** — S4's biggest slice: GitTree could already run all four operations, but every real git flag behind them was unreachable. All four adopt IntelliJ's **"Modify options"** pattern (a new shared `ModifyOptions.tsx`): a compact primary form plus a `Modify options ▾` dropdown of the actual flags, each ticked option revealing its control — and **un-ticking an option also turns the flag off**, so a flag the user cannot see can never survive in the command. Conflicting combinations are reported *with reasons* under the form and disable the action, rather than being silently greyed out. **Merge:** ref picker + `--no-ff`, `--ff-only`, `--squash`, `--no-commit`, `--allow-unrelated-histories`, `--strategy`/`--strategy-option` (separate controls) and a custom `-m`; validation rejects `--no-ff`+`--ff-only`, `--squash`+`--no-ff` (git's own refusal), and a message a squash would silently drop. **Rebase:** onto-ref + an optional From ref that turns the command into a `--onto` transplant, plus `--interactive`, `--rebase-merges`, `--autostash`, `--no-verify`, `--keep-empty`; ticking Interactive renames the action to `Start Interactive Rebase` and hands off to **P5's** editor instead of git's terminal editor, and says so in the dialog. **`--preserve-merges` is deliberately absent** despite the phase plan listing it: git deleted it in 2.36, so offering it would build a command that simply fails. **Reset:** a ref picker *and* a free-text revision box (so `HEAD~3` and raw hashes work), the four-mode radio **`Soft · Mixed · Hard · Keep`**, and a live mode-specific impact line — "unstages 2 staged changes / files on disk untouched" vs "**DISCARDS** 3 local changes" vs "keeps your changes — git refuses the reset instead of discarding them". Only `--hard` takes the destructive confirmation (`--keep` cannot lose work by definition); both snapshot via P9, and `--keep`'s refusal is surfaced as git's own reason, not a stack trace. **Rollback:** a checkbox `FileTree` with `N files` counts and status colors, `[x] Delete local copies of added files` as a **real switch** (unticked, a staged-new file is unstaged but stays on disk), and an `InlineDiff` preview of what would be reverted; the plan is computed by the pure `rollbackPlan.ts` — tracked changes restore from HEAD (a rename's source comes back too), staged-new files unstage, and deletions happen only when the box is ticked. Every dialog shows the dim "will run" line built from the same argv the host executes; rollback's line additionally names the deletion count, because deletions have no git command to show. New: `gittree.merge.dialog`, `gittree.rebase.dialog`, `gittree.reset.dialog`, `gittree.rollback.dialog`, a shared `RefPicker.tsx`, and `refs.ts` reading the picker's refs from the already-maintained model snapshot. Tests: **+43 unit** (one per flag across all four builders, the mutually-exclusive pairs, the rename-source and delete-checkbox rules, and the impact-line wording), **+19 Playwright** (compact-by-default, reveal-a-flag, the two rejection cases *with their reasons*, un-tick-clears-the-flag, the strategy pair, the `--onto` transplant, the interactive hand-off note, the live impact line across all four modes, a raw revision expression, and rollback's counts/preview/plan/disabled-when-empty), **+10 integration** (`--squash` leaves the change **staged, not committed**; `--no-ff` produces a real merge commit where a fast-forward was possible; **a rejected option set runs nothing at all**; rebase replays onto a new base with the local commit rewritten; **`Reset Keep` refuses** and loses nothing while `--hard` from the same state moves HEAD *and* snapshots; a soft reset keeps the index; **rollback of 2 of 5 files leaves the other 3 untouched**; added-file copies are deleted **only** when ticked; the preview diffs worktree↔HEAD and reports whole-file for a file absent from HEAD). **Bug the Playwright layer caught:** three Merge handlers read `e.currentTarget.value` *inside* a `setState` updater — React nulls `currentTarget` before the updater runs, so the dialog threw and unmounted mid-interaction. Fixed by hoisting the read; audited every other dialog handler (all already synchronous). Gates: typecheck+lint clean, 575 unit, 162 webview + 3 skipped, 183 integration passing + 2 pending — the only red remains the pre-existing P7-T2 500 ms stash bound already flagged in the nightlog.

P13-T4 ✅ 2026-08-23 — **Cherry-pick · Stash · Unstash · Apply Patch · Clone · Manage Remotes** — **S4 CLOSED**: all twelve flows in the gap analysis now have option-bearing dialogs, each previewing the exact argv it will run. **Cherry-pick:** a checkbox commit list (drawn from `--branches --not HEAD`, or seeded from a log selection) plus `-x`, `--no-commit` and `-m <parent>` — the mainline field is offered *only* for a commit that really has two parents, and the picked commits are re-sorted **oldest-first** before reaching git whatever order they were ticked in. `-x` is dropped automatically when `--no-commit` means no commit exists to carry it. **Stash:** message + `--keep-index`, `--include-untracked`, `--all` and `--staged`; `--staged` is disabled with a *stated reason* on a git older than 2.35, and ticking it clears what it excludes rather than leaving a default only the validator would notice. **Unstash:** the stash list, `[x] Pop stash`, `[ ] Reinstate index`, a target-changelist selector wired to P3's `getLists`/`moveFiles`, and an `InlineDiff` of the selected file **inside** the stash (`stash@{n}^` ↔ `stash@{n}`). **Apply Patch:** file picker → pre-apply summary from `splitPatchFiles` (files, hunk counts, and a **missing-file warning** for paths the patch edits that are not in the worktree) → `--3way`, `--reverse`, `-p<n>`, `--whitespace=fix`, plus "apply as content, not commits" for a mailbox patch; the preview line switches between `git am -3` and `git apply …` exactly as the engine will. Rejects come back as a file list. **Clone:** URL + directory with the destination auto-filled from the URL *until the user edits it*, `--recurse-submodules`, `--depth`, `--branch`, an after-clone choice (new window / add to workspace / nothing), and the target directory validated as empty **before** the button is live — non-empty is blocked with the count. URL entry only (deviation D11). **Manage Remotes** — the one with no predecessor at all, there was no remote-management command in GitTree: a name/fetch/push table with add, edit, remove and `git remote prune`, applying live over RPC the way IntelliJ's does; an empty push URL reads as "same as fetch" instead of echoing the fetch URL, `planRemoteEdit` renames *before* touching URLs so later commands address the new name, and clearing a push URL uses `set-url --push --delete` rather than setting it empty. Also new: **`dialogRegistry.ts`**, the single table P13-T7's operations popup will enumerate — command, label, group, accelerator and an `applies(ctx)` predicate — so a dialog added later cannot silently go missing from the hub (the gap analysis §1 blames exactly that kind of hand-kept inventory). Tests: **+43 unit** (one per flag across cherry-pick/stash/clone/remote builders, the exclusivity rules, `suggestedDirName` incl. scp-style remotes, `planRemoteEdit`'s four cases, and the registry's context gating), **+21 Playwright** (oldest-first ordering, the merge-only mainline field, `--all` hiding untracked, `--staged` exclusivity, unstash entry-switching and preview, the patch summary + missing-file warning + `am`↔`apply` preview switch, clone's auto-fill/stop-on-edit and the non-empty block, and remotes **add → edit → remove round-tripping** plus duplicate/illegal-name refusal before the host is called), **+9 integration** (cherry-pick `-x` really appends the source line and `--no-commit` stages without committing; stash `--keep-index` leaves the staged half on disk while the unstaged edit goes; unstash lists the entry's files, restores them and pop drops the entry; apply-patch **reverse undoes a previously applied patch**; a patch touching a missing file is flagged before applying; clone into a non-empty directory is blocked with the reason while a fresh one clones for real from the local bare repo; Manage Remotes add → prune → remove round-trips and a duplicate is refused before git sees it). **Test lesson:** two of my own tests passed in isolation and failed in the full run — `refreshNow()` can resolve against a refresh that *started before* the write under test, so reading `model.snapshot` straight afterwards is a race. Replaced with a `waitForStatus()` helper that waits for the fact; applied to every snapshot-dependent assertion in the suite. Gates: typecheck+lint clean, 618 unit, 183 webview + 3 skipped, **192 integration passing + 2 pending**, the only red still the pre-existing P7-T2 500 ms stash bound.

P13-T6 ✅ 2026-08-23 — **Worktree management** (gap analysis §3.10). GitTree already resolved worktree paths internally through `commondir`, but had **no management UI and no create/remove commands at all** — which is also why the reference branch submenu's `New Worktree from 'X'…` could not exist. Now: a `parseWorktreeList` parser for `git worktree list --porcelain` (blank-line records, `bare`/`detached`/`locked`/`prunable` with their optional reasons, unknown keys ignored rather than fatal), a `WorktreeService` over add/list/remove/lock/unlock/prune, the **`gittree.worktrees` tree view** (branch or `detached at <sha>` as the label, path as the description, `repo`/`lock`/`warning`/`git-branch` icons, main-vs-linked and locked-vs-unlocked in the `contextValue` so the row menu can gate on them), and the **New Worktree dialog** — three branch modes (check out an existing branch · create a new one from a start point · detach at a revision), the location field defaulting to IntelliJ's sibling `../<repo>-<branch>` **with the branch's slashes flattened** so the result is one directory rather than a nested tree, `[x] Open in new window`, and `--no-checkout` / `--force` behind Modify options. The location follows the chosen branch until the user edits it, then stops. Removal of a **dirty** worktree is git's refusal, surfaced verbatim, with `--force` as the explicit escape hatch; the main worktree cannot be removed at all. **Compatibility finding worth knowing:** `locked` and `prunable` only reached `worktree list --porcelain` in **git 2.36**, and this machine runs **2.28** — so on the user's own build the view would never show a lock badge and prune's stale records would be invisible. Rather than ship a feature that silently does nothing here, `augmentFromDisk` derives both from git's own on-disk layout (a linked worktree's `.git` is a pointer file to its admin directory; a lock is a `locked` file there whose contents are the reason; a missing directory is prunable by definition). It only ever *adds* information, so a newer git keeps what it reported and the two agree. Recorded as deviation **D16**, revisitable once git 2.36 is the floor. Tests: **+10 unit** on the parser (ordinary, bare, detached, locked-with-and-without-reason, prunable, CRLF, unknown keys, spaces in paths, main-is-first), **+19 unit** on the argv (every mode and flag, the validation rules, and `defaultWorktreePath`'s slash-flattening and separator-style preservation), **+6 Playwright** (seed branch and default path, path-follows-branch-until-edited, `-b` mode keeping the branch as start point, illegal branch names refused with a reason, detach's free-text revision, and the accepted payload), **+5 integration** (the main worktree is listed first and marked main; **create → list → lock → unlock → remove** round-trips with the flags read back each time; **removing a dirty worktree is refused with git's own reason** and `--force` then succeeds; **prune clears a worktree whose directory vanished**, with `prunable` observed first; the tree view yields one leaf row per worktree with the right label and context value). **Test lesson:** `os.tmpdir()` hands back the Windows 8.3 short path while git prints the resolved long form, so every path comparison failed — the suite now canonicalizes its temp root with `realpathSync.native` once at setup. Also raised the suite teardown's timeout to 180 s: it does two *retrying* git config writes (up to ~22 s each under the lock contention `gitWithRetry` exists for) plus a recursive delete of a tree holding a bare repo, a clone and worktrees, and 30 s was optimistic enough to fail the whole gate under load. Gates: typecheck+lint clean, 647 unit, 189 webview + 3 skipped, **198 integration passing + 2 pending** — the only red the pre-existing P7-T2 500 ms stash bound. (One earlier full run also hit the documented P12 symref flake, cascading into four follow-on P12 failures; it did not recur.)

P13-T5 ✅ 2026-08-23 — **Branches popup → action hub** (gap analysis S3 · §2A "Branches popup" / "Branch row submenu expanded") — **and the real defect fixed**. Still a native `QuickPick`: master §6 chose it over a webview for genuine focus, search and keyboard parity, and the reference screenshot vindicates that — its field reads *"Search for branches **and actions**"*. The gap was content and structure, not the widget. **Top-level verbs come first**, before any branch: `Update Project… Ctrl+T` · `Commit… Ctrl+K` · `Push… Ctrl+Shift+K` · `New Branch… Ctrl+Alt+N` · `Checkout Tag or Revision…`, each carrying keywords so typing `merge` finds Update Project rather than only branches. **Folder rows** fold a prefix (`p13hub  2 branches`); picking one drills into a scoped popup with a `‹ Back` row and a breadcrumb title, and **typing collapses the folders and searches flat across every level**, so drill-in can never trap anyone (deviation D5 — `QuickPick` cannot render a collapsible tree). Favorites and Recent stay flat by design: they are short curated lists where a folder row would hide the very branch the user pinned. Header buttons per §2A: new branch, fetch, the **incoming/outgoing toggle** (persisted to a new `gittree.branches.showIncomingOutgoing`, which also flips the ↓/↑ order) and a **gear** that opens the branch settings. The `›` per-row submenu is built from `branchActions.ts` — the single registry the popup, the branches tree view and the P12-T2 log Branches pane all consume — with separators between the reference's groups and the labels interpolated with real names. **The defect (T5.5):** `branchOps.mergeIntoCurrent` had **exactly one call site**, the branches tree's drag-and-drop handler, so merging a branch into the current one was reachable *only by dragging*. It now has a registry entry, a submenu row, a tree context-menu row and the command `gittree.branch.mergeIntoCurrent`. Four more actions the reference has and GitTree lacked came with it: `Rebase '<current>' onto 'X'` (the mirror of the existing checkout-and-rebase, new `BranchOps.rebaseCurrentOnto`), `Pull into '<current>' Using Merge` and `Delete 'origin/x' on Remote` for remote rows (both new ops; the remote delete is behind an explicit confirmation, being the one branch action other people can see), and `New Worktree from 'X'…` wired to P13-T6. Every registry action is now dispatchable by id from any surface via `gittree.branch.*`, and an action that does not apply **reports instead of running**. Keybindings gated on the IntelliJ keymap: `Ctrl+Alt+N` → New Branch, `F2` → rename in the branches view.
